If only f3 is safe, and it's my turn, I must move the king to f3? No, that's only if I'm in check or have no other moves. But wait, if all other squares are attacked, and I'm NOT in check, I can still move to f3, but I could also just not move? No, you must make a move.
But why is f4 not listed as a move? Because you can't "move" to the square you are already on.
So the only legal move is to a square that is not attacked.
If all other squares (e3, e4, e5, f5, g5, g4, g3) are attacked, then f3 is the only legal move.

- e4: Attacked by Queen e6 (file e) and King d4 (adjacent). Yes.
- e5: Attacked by King d4 (adjacent). Yes.

So indeed, f3 is the only legal move.
